Status: COMPLETE
Reports verified on disk:
- building-selection-brackets/SURFACE_DRAWLINE_ABUFFER_ZTEST_PIXEL_CONTRACT_GHIDRA_REPORT.md
- building-selection-brackets/TACTICAL_DRAWLINE3D_BRACKET_DEPTH_ARGUMENTS_GHIDRA_REPORT.md

Reconciliation summary:
- Selected building brackets are primary-surface line pixels, not final UI overlay pixels. Candidate pixels clip before rasterization, then require strict `(uint16)line_depth < *g_ZBufferPixel` and `g_ABufferPixel != 0`.
- A-buffer values are now implementation-ready: visible/neutral reset is `0x7F`, `0x00` suppresses, nonzero/non-`0x7F` modulates channels, and `0xFE` is source-side transparent/skip for shroud/fog assets.
- Fog A-buffer blending is conditional and off in standard YR (`FogOfWar=no`), while standard shroud and enemy-gap-induced re-shroud feed the normal A-buffer writer.
- The final `DrawLine3D` argument is the surface Z-write flag, not line depth. Selected building bracket callers pass `0`, so brackets Z-test but do not write depth.
- Bracket Z-test samples global tactical `g_ZBuffer`/`g_ABuffer`. Terrain/TMP writes that Z-buffer after dirty clear; normal building SHP bodies do not populate the bracket-sampled Z-buffer in the normal selected-building route.
- Runtime fixtures for parity are: ordinary `GACNST`/SHP body silhouette, shroud-edge suppression/dimming, `[GAGAP]` gap-induced re-shroud/gap visual field, overlapping selected buildings with the second `DrawExtras` pass, and gate/VXL-style first-pass art between early and final front brackets.

Cross-doc contradictions / stale wording:
- `TECHNO_DRAWBEHIND_BUILDING_BRACKET_EDGES_GHIDRA_REPORT.md` and `TECHNO_DRAWEXTRAS_BUILDING_BRACKET_BLOCK_GHIDRA_REPORT.md` still contain older wording treating the fifth `DrawLine3D` argument as a depth-like input. The current slot-2 report resolves this: endpoint depths are computed internally from endpoint Z, and the final argument is the Z-write flag.

Spot checks performed:
- Ghidra read-only decompile of `0x004BFD30` corroborated the strict Z-test, A-buffer `0` suppression, `0x7F` neutral fast path, modulation for other values, and guarded Z-write.
- Ghidra read-only decompile of `0x006DBB60` corroborated projection through `WorldToScreenSub`, screen-Y `AdjustForZ`, and dispatch to `g_PrimarySurface+0x34`; assembly-level details in the report resolve the decompiler's ambiguous final-argument expression.
- Source data check confirmed `ini/rulesmd.ini` has `FogOfWar=no` and `[GAGAP] GapGenerator=yes` / `SuperGapRadiusInCells=10`.

No code or source-doc patches were applied during this reconciliation per re-swarm rules.

## Slot 4 - UnitClass::Receive_Radio(0x16) second-call scheduling

- Status: COMPLETE
- Report: `docs/research/UNITCLASS_RECEIVE_RADIO_0X16_SECOND_CALL_SCHEDULING_GHIDRA_REPORT.md`
- Claim: `0x16` does not self-schedule. First ordinary `0x16` calls locomotor/facing `+0x4C(0x4000)` and returns `1`; building treats return `1` as success and performs no immediate retry or fallback sound. The later/aligned `0x16` is caused by a later `Mission_Enter -> BuildingClass::Receive_Radio(0x0E)` resend when unit-side `0x12` returns already-there `0x14`, so the building sends `0x18` then `0x16` again.
- Key evidence: `UnitClass::Receive_Radio @ 0x00737430` assembly `0x007376BF..0x00737780`; `BuildingClass::Receive_Radio @ 0x0043C2D0` assembly `0x0043CAAE..0x0043CAF7`; `FootClass::Mission_Enter @ 0x004D9290` assembly `0x004D92B2..0x004D92BF`, `0x004D946C..0x004D9497`; `FootClass::Receive_Radio(0x12) @ 0x004D8FB0` assembly `0x004D9140..0x004D9197`.
- Handoff: Rust should add/preserve a first-`0x16` sync/turn phase that does not unload, then wait for stock MissionEnter retry cadence (`[Enter] Rate=.016` -> `14..16` frames) before the building resend can deliver the second `0x16`.
